1340:
1341:    /// <summary>
1342:    /// Sends a notification to the restaurant when a booking is modified.
1343:    /// </summary>
1344:    private async Task SendModificationNotificationAsync(
1345:        BookingRecord originalBooking,
1346:        BookingRecord updatedBooking,
1347:        BookingUpdateData changes,
1348:        string changeDescription,
1349:        CancellationToken ct)
1350:    {
1351:        try
1352:        {
1353:            var sb = new StringBuilder();
1354:            sb.AppendLine("🔔 *Reserva modificada por Asistente de IA de Villa Carmen*");
1355:            sb.AppendLine();
1356:            sb.AppendLine("📋 *INFORMACIÓN DE LA RESERVA:*");
1357:            sb.AppendLine($"👤 Nombre: {updatedBooking.CustomerName}");
1358:            sb.AppendLine($"📞 Teléfono: {updatedBooking.ContactPhone}");
1359:            sb.AppendLine($"📅 Fecha: {updatedBooking.DateFormatted} ({updatedBooking.DayName})");
1360:            sb.AppendLine($"🕐 Hora: {updatedBooking.TimeFormatted}");
1361:            sb.AppendLine($"👥 Personas: {updatedBooking.PartySize}");
1362:
1363:            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(updatedBooking.ArrozType))
1364:            {
1365:                sb.AppendLine($"🍚 Arroz: {updatedBooking.ArrozType} ({updatedBooking.ArrozServings} raciones)");
1366:            }
1367:            else
1368:            {
1369:                sb.AppendLine("🍚 Arroz: Sin arroz");
1370:            }
1371:
1372:            sb.AppendLine($"🪑 Tronas: {updatedBooking.HighChairs}");
1373:            sb.AppendLine($"🛒 Carritos: {updatedBooking.BabyStrollers}");
1374:            sb.AppendLine();
1375:            sb.AppendLine("✏️ *CAMBIOS REALIZADOS:*");
1376:
1377:            // Show before/after for each changed field
1378:            if (changes.ReservationDate != null)
1379:            {
1380:                sb.AppendLine($"📅 Fecha: {originalBooking.DateFormatted} → {updatedBooking.DateFormatted}");
1381:            }
1382:
1383:            if (changes.ReservationTime != null)
1384:            {
1385:                sb.AppendLine($"🕐 Hora: {originalBooking.TimeFormatted} → {updatedBooking.TimeFormatted}");
1386:            }
1387:
1388:            if (changes.PartySize.HasValue)
1389:            {
1390:                sb.AppendLine($"👥 Personas: {originalBooking.PartySize} → {updatedBooking.PartySize}");
1391:            }
1392:
1393:            if (changes.ClearRice)
1394:            {
1395:                var originalRice = originalBooking.ArrozType != null
1396:                    ? $"{originalBooking.ArrozType} ({originalBooking.ArrozServings} raciones)"
1397:                    : "Sin arroz";
1398:                sb.AppendLine($"🍚 Arroz: {originalRice} → Sin arroz");
1399:            }
1400:            else if (changes.ArrozType != null || changes.ArrozServings.HasValue)
1401:            {
1402:                var originalRice = originalBooking.ArrozType != null
1403:                    ? $"{originalBooking.ArrozType} ({originalBooking.ArrozServings} raciones)"
1404:                    : "Sin arroz";
1405:                var newRice = updatedBooking.ArrozType != null
1406:                    ? $"{updatedBooking.ArrozType} ({updatedBooking.ArrozServings} raciones)"
1407:                    : "Sin arroz";
1408:                sb.AppendLine($"🍚 Arroz: {originalRice} → {newRice}");
1409:            }
1410:
1411:            if (changes.HighChairs.HasValue)
1412:            {
1413:                sb.AppendLine($"🪑 Tronas: {originalBooking.HighChairs} → {updatedBooking.HighChairs}");
1414:            }
1415:
1416:            if (changes.BabyStrollers.HasValue)
1417:            {
1418:                sb.AppendLine($"🛒 Carritos: {originalBooking.BabyStrollers} → {updatedBooking.BabyStrollers}");
1419:            }
1420:
1421:            sb.AppendLine();
1422:            sb.AppendLine($"🆔 ID Reserva: {updatedBooking.Id}");
1423:
1424:            var message = sb.ToString();
1425:            foreach (var phone in ManagementPhones)
1426:            {
1427:                try
1428:                {
1429:                    await _whatsAppService.SendTextAsync(phone, message, ct);
1430:                    _logger.LogDebug("Sent modification notification to {Phone}", phone);
1431:                }
1432:                catch (Exception phoneEx)
1433:                {
1434:                    _logger.LogError(phoneEx, "Failed to send modification notification to {Phone}", phone);
1435:                }
1436:            }
1437:
1438:            _logger.LogInformation(
1439:                "Sent modification notification for booking {BookingId} to management team",
1440:                updatedBooking.Id);
1441:        }
1442:        catch (Exception ex)
1443:        {
1444:            // Log but don't fail the modification if notification fails
1445:            _logger.LogError(ex,
1446:                "Failed to send modification notification for booking {BookingId}",
1447:                updatedBooking.Id);
1448:        }
1449:    }
1450:
1451:    /// <summary>
1452:    /// Syncs booking modifications to the external PHP system via HTTP API.
1453:    /// This ensures the external restaurant system is updated with changes made through the bot.
1454:    /// </summary>
1455:    private async Task SyncModificationToExternalSystemAsync(
1456:        int bookingId,
1457:        BookingUpdateData changes,
1458:        CancellationToken ct)
1459:    {
1460:        try
1461:        {
1462:            // Map BookingUpdateData fields to external API calls
1463:            var tasks = new List<Task<bool>>();
1464:
1465:            if (changes.ReservationDate != null)
1466:            {
1467:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1468:                    bookingId, "reservation_date", changes.ReservationDate, ct));
1469:            }
1470:
1471:            if (changes.ReservationTime != null)
1472:            {
1473:                // Format time as HH:MM:SS
1474:                var timeValue = changes.ReservationTime;
1475:                if (!timeValue.Contains(":"))
1476:                {
1477:                    timeValue = timeValue + ":00";
1478:                }
1479:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1480:                    bookingId, "reservation_time", timeValue, ct));
1481:            }
1482:
1483:            if (changes.PartySize.HasValue)
1484:            {
1485:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1486:                    bookingId, "party_size", changes.PartySize.Value.ToString(), ct));
1487:            }
1488:
1489:            if (changes.ClearRice)
1490:            {
1491:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1492:                    bookingId, "rice_type", "", ct));
1493:            }
1494:            else if (changes.ArrozType != null)
1495:            {
1496:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1497:                    bookingId, "rice_type", changes.ArrozType, ct));
1498:            }
1499:
1500:            if (changes.ArrozServings.HasValue)
1501:            {
1502:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1503:                    bookingId, "arroz_servings", changes.ArrozServings.Value.ToString(), ct));
1504:            }
1505:
1506:            if (changes.HighChairs.HasValue)
1507:            {
1508:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1509:                    bookingId, "high_chairs", changes.HighChairs.Value.ToString(), ct));
1510:            }
1511:
1512:            if (changes.BabyStrollers.HasValue)
1513:            {
1514:                tasks.Add(_externalReservationService.UpdateReservationFieldAsync(
1515:                    bookingId, "baby_strollers", changes.BabyStrollers.Value.ToString(), ct));
1516:            }
1517:
1518:            // Execute all updates in parallel
1519:            if (tasks.Count > 0)
1520:            {
1521:                var results = await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
1522:                var successCount = results.Count(r => r);
1523:                
1524:                _logger.LogInformation(
1525:                    "External sync for booking {BookingId}: {Success}/{Total} fields updated",
1526:                    bookingId, successCount, tasks.Count);
1527:            }
1528:            else
1529:            {
1530:                _logger.LogDebug(
1531:                    "No fields to sync to external system for booking {BookingId}",
1532:                    bookingId);
1533:            }
1534:        }
1535:        catch (Exception ex)
1536:        {
1537:            // Log but don't fail - local DB update already succeeded
1538:            _logger.LogWarning(ex,
1539:                "Failed to sync some fields to external system for booking {BookingId}",
1540:                bookingId);
1541:        }
1542:    }
1543:
1544:    #endregion
1545:
1546:    #region Legacy Method (kept for backwards compatibility)
1547:
1548:    /// <summary>
1549:    /// Legacy entry point - redirects to new ProcessModificationAsync.
1550:    /// </summary>
1551:    public async Task<AgentResponse> StartModificationFlowAsync(
1552:        string senderNumber,
1553:        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
1554:    {
1555:        var message = new WhatsAppMessage
1556:        {
1557:            SenderNumber = senderNumber,
1558:            PushName = "Cliente",
1559:            MessageText = "",
1560:            MessageType = "text"
1561:        };

1563:        return await ProcessModificationAsync(message, null, cancellationToken);
1564:    }
1565:
1566:    #endregion
1567:
1568:    #region Rice Modification Shortcut
1569:
1570:    /// <summary>
1571:    /// Starts a modification flow pre-configured for rice changes.
1572:    /// Skips the "what do you want to modify?" step.
1573:    /// </summary>
1574:    public async Task<AgentResponse> StartRiceModificationAsync(
1575:        WhatsAppMessage message,
1576:        BookingRecord booking,
1577:        string? preExtractedRiceType,
1578:        int? preExtractedServings,
1579:        CancellationToken ct = default)
1580:    {
1581:        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(preExtractedRiceType) && IsGenericRiceReference(preExtractedRiceType))
1582:        {
1583:            preExtractedRiceType = null;
1584:        }
1585:
1586:        _logger.LogInformation(
1587:            "Starting rice modification for booking {BookingId}, pre-extracted rice: {Rice}, servings: {Servings}",
1588:            booking.Id, preExtractedRiceType ?? "(none)", preExtractedServings?.ToString() ?? "N/A");
1589:
1590:        // If rice type was extracted, validate it first
1591:        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(preExtractedRiceType))
1592:        {
1593:            var validation = await _riceValidator.ValidateAsync(
1594:                preExtractedRiceType, "villacarmen", ct);
1595:
1596:            if (validation.IsValid && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(validation.RiceName))
1597:            {
1598:                // Valid rice - check if we also have servings
1599:                if (preExtractedServings.HasValue && preExtractedServings.Value >= 2)
1600:                {
1601:                    // We have both rice and valid servings - go straight to confirmation
1602:                    var pendingChanges = new BookingUpdateData
1603:                    {
1604:                        ArrozType = validation.RiceName,
1605:                        ArrozServings = preExtractedServings.Value
1606:                    };
1607:
1608:                    var state = new ModificationState
1609:                    {
1610:                        PhoneNumber = message.SenderNumber,
1611:                        Stage = ModificationStage.AwaitingConfirmation,
1612:                        FoundBookings = new List<BookingRecord> { booking },
1613:                        SelectedBooking = booking,
1614:                        FieldToModify = "rice",
1615:                        PendingChanges = pendingChanges,
1616:                        ChangeDescription = $"añadir {validation.RiceName} ({preExtractedServings.Value} raciones)"
1617:                    };
1618:                    _stateStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, state);
1619:
1620:                    var currentRice = string.IsNullOrEmpty(booking.ArrozType)
1621:                        ? "sin arroz"
1622:                        : $"{booking.ArrozType} ({booking.ArrozServings} raciones)";
1623:
1624:                    return new AgentResponse
1625:                    {
1626:                        Intent = IntentType.Modification,
1627:                        AiResponse = $"Vas a {state.ChangeDescription} a tu reserva del {booking.DateFormatted} (actualmente {currentRice}). ¿Confirmas? (Sí/No)"
1628:                    };
1629:                }
1630:
1631:                // Valid rice but need servings
1632:                var stateNeedServings = new ModificationState
1633:                {
1634:                    PhoneNumber = message.SenderNumber,
1635:                    Stage = ModificationStage.CollectingNewValue,
1636:                    FoundBookings = new List<BookingRecord> { booking },
1637:                    SelectedBooking = booking,
1638:                    FieldToModify = "rice",
1639:                    PendingChanges = new BookingUpdateData { ArrozType = validation.RiceName }
1640:                };
1641:                _stateStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, stateNeedServings);
1642:
1643:                var currentRiceInfo = string.IsNullOrEmpty(booking.ArrozType)
1644:                    ? "sin arroz"
1645:                    : $"{booking.ArrozType} ({booking.ArrozServings} raciones)";
1646:
1647:                return new AgentResponse
1648:                {
1649:                    Intent = IntentType.Modification,
1650:                    AiResponse = $"Perfecto, {validation.RiceName} disponible.\n\n" +
1651:                                $"Tu reserva del {booking.DateFormatted}: actualmente {currentRiceInfo}.\n\n" +
1652:                                $"¿Cuántas raciones queréis? (mínimo 2, máximo {booking.PartySize})"
1653:                };
1654:            }
1655:
1656:            if (validation.Status == "multiple" && validation.Options?.Count > 0)
1657:            {
1658:                // Multiple matches - ask user to choose
1659:                var state = new ModificationState
1660:                {
1661:                    PhoneNumber = message.SenderNumber,
1662:                    Stage = ModificationStage.CollectingNewValue,
1663:                    FoundBookings = new List<BookingRecord> { booking },
1664:                    SelectedBooking = booking,
1665:                    FieldToModify = "rice"
1666:                };
1667:                _stateStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, state);
1668:
1669:                var options = string.Join("\n", validation.Options.Select((r, i) => $"{i + 1}. {r}"));
1670:                return new AgentResponse
1671:                {
1672:                    Intent = IntentType.Modification,
1673:                    AiResponse = $"He encontrado varias opciones de arroz. ¿Cuál prefieres?\n\n{options}"
1674:                };
1675:            }
1676:
1677:            // Invalid rice
1678:            var invalidState = new ModificationState
1679:            {
1680:                PhoneNumber = message.SenderNumber,
1681:                Stage = ModificationStage.CollectingNewValue,
1682:                FoundBookings = new List<BookingRecord> { booking },
1683:                SelectedBooking = booking,
1684:                FieldToModify = "rice"
1685:            };
1686:            _stateStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, invalidState);
1687:
1688:            return new AgentResponse
1689:            {
1690:                Intent = IntentType.Modification,
1691:                AiResponse = validation.Message ??
1692:                    "No tenemos ese arroz. Puedes ver la carta en: https://alqueriavillacarmen.com/menufindesemana.php\n\n¿Qué arroz te gustaría añadir?"
1693:            };
1694:        }
1695:
1696:        // No rice type extracted - set up for rice modification and ask
1697:        var modState = new ModificationState
1698:        {
1699:            PhoneNumber = message.SenderNumber,
1700:            Stage = ModificationStage.CollectingNewValue,
1701:            FoundBookings = new List<BookingRecord> { booking },
1702:            SelectedBooking = booking,
1703:            FieldToModify = "rice"
1704:        };
1705:        _stateStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, modState);
1706:
1707:        var currentRiceInfoGeneric = string.IsNullOrEmpty(booking.ArrozType)
1708:            ? "Actualmente no tienes arroz en la reserva."
1709:            : $"Actualmente tienes {booking.ArrozType} ({booking.ArrozServings} raciones).";
1710:
1711:        return new AgentResponse
1712:        {
1713:            Intent = IntentType.Modification,
1714:            AiResponse = $"{currentRiceInfoGeneric}\n\n¿Qué arroz te gustaría? Puedes ver la carta en: https://alqueriavillacarmen.com/menufindesemana.php"
1715:        };
1716:    }
1717:
1718:    /// <summary>
1719:    /// Starts a rice modification flow when user has multiple bookings.
1720:    /// Asks which booking to modify first, storing the pre-extracted rice info.
1721:    /// </summary>
1722:    public Task<AgentResponse> StartRiceModificationWithSelectionAsync(
1723:        WhatsAppMessage message,
1724:        List<BookingRecord> bookings,
1725:        string? preExtractedRiceType,
1726:        int? preExtractedServings,
1727:        CancellationToken ct = default)
1728:    {
1729:        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(preExtractedRiceType) && IsGenericRiceReference(preExtractedRiceType))
1730:        {
1731:            preExtractedRiceType = null;
1732:            preExtractedServings = null;
1733:        }
1734:
1735:        _logger.LogInformation(
1736:            "Starting rice modification with selection for {Phone}, {Count} bookings, pre-extracted rice: {Rice}",
1737:            message.SenderNumber, bookings.Count, preExtractedRiceType ?? "(none)");
1738:
1739:        // Store state with pre-extracted rice info for later use
1740:        var state = new ModificationState
1741:        {
1742:            PhoneNumber = message.SenderNumber,
1743:            Stage = ModificationStage.SelectingBooking,
1744:            FoundBookings = bookings,
1745:            FieldToModify = "rice", // Pre-select rice field
1746:            // Store extracted rice info in PendingChanges for later
1747:            PendingChanges = preExtractedRiceType != null
1748:                ? new BookingUpdateData 
1749:                { 
1750:                    ArrozType = preExtractedRiceType,
1751:                    ArrozServings = preExtractedServings
1752:                }
1753:                : null
1754:        };
1755:        _stateStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, state);
1756:
1757:        // Build booking selection response
1758:        var sb = new StringBuilder();
1759:        sb.AppendLine("Tienes varias reservas activas. ¿A cuál quieres añadir arroz?");
1760:        sb.AppendLine();
1761:
1762:        for (int i = 0; i < bookings.Count; i++)
1763:        {
1764:            var b = bookings[i];
1765:            var riceInfo = string.IsNullOrEmpty(b.ArrozType)
1766:                ? "sin arroz"
1767:                : $"{b.ArrozType} ({b.ArrozServings} raciones)";
1768:            sb.AppendLine($"{i + 1}. *{b.DateFormatted}* a las *{b.TimeFormatted}* ({b.PartySize} personas, {riceInfo})");
1769:        }
1770:
1771:        sb.AppendLine();
1772:        sb.Append("Responde con el número o di algo como \"la del sábado\"");
1773:
1774:        return Task.FromResult(new AgentResponse
1775:        {
1776:            Intent = IntentType.Modification,
1777:            AiResponse = sb.ToString()
1778:        });
1779:    }

  82:

 135:            }
 136:
 137:            // ========== CHECK FOR RICE OFFER RESPONSE ==========
 138:            // If user is responding to a rice offer message for an existing reservation,
 139:            // handle the response directly without going through AI
 140:            var riceOfferResponse = await CheckRiceOfferResponseAsync(
 141:                originalMessage, cancellationToken);
 142:            if (riceOfferResponse != null)
 143:            {
 144:                return riceOfferResponse;
 145:            }
 146:
 147:            // ========== CHECK FOR ACTIVE MODIFICATION SESSION ==========
 148:            // If user has an active modification session, route ALL messages to the handler
 149:            // This ensures multi-turn modification flows work correctly
 150:            var modificationState = _modificationStateStore.Get(originalMessage.SenderNumber);
 151:            if (modificationState != null || mainAgentResponse.Intent == IntentType.Modification)
 152:            {
 153:                _logger.LogInformation(
 154:                    "Routing to modification handler (activeSession={HasSession}, intent={Intent})",
 155:                    modificationState != null, mainAgentResponse.Intent);
 156:
 157:                return await HandleModificationAsync(
 158:                    mainAgentResponse, originalMessage, modificationState, cancellationToken);
 159:            }
 160:
 161:            // ========== CHECK FOR ACTIVE CANCELLATION SESSION ==========
 162:            // If user has an active cancellation session, route ALL messages to the handler
 163:            // This ensures multi-turn cancellation flows work correctly
 164:            var cancellationState = _cancellationStateStore.Get(originalMessage.SenderNumber);
 165:            if (cancellationState != null || mainAgentResponse.Intent == IntentType.Cancellation)
 166:            {
 167:                _logger.LogInformation(
 168:                    "Routing to cancellation handler (activeSession={HasSession}, intent={Intent})",
 169:                    cancellationState != null, mainAgentResponse.Intent);
 170:
 171:                return await HandleCancellationAsync(
 172:                    mainAgentResponse, originalMessage, cancellationState, cancellationToken);
 173:            }
 174:
 175:            // ========== CHECK FOR PENDING RICE SELECTION ==========
 176:            // If user was asked to choose between multiple rice options, process their selection
 177:            var pendingRice = _pendingRiceStore.Get(originalMessage.SenderNumber);
 178:            if (pendingRice != null)
 179:            {
 180:                var selectedRice = TryParseRiceSelection(originalMessage.MessageText, pendingRice.Options);
 181:                if (selectedRice != null)
 182:                {
 183:                    _logger.LogInformation(
 184:                        "User selected rice: {Rice} from options",
 185:                        selectedRice);
 186:
 187:                    // Clear pending rice selection
 188:                    _pendingRiceStore.Clear(originalMessage.SenderNumber);
 189:
 190:                    // Update the pending booking with selected rice
 191:                    var pendingBooking = _pendingBookingStore.Get(originalMessage.SenderNumber);
 192:                    if (pendingBooking != null)
 193:                    {
 194:                        var updatedBooking = pendingBooking with { ArrozType = selectedRice };
 195:                        _pendingBookingStore.Set(originalMessage.SenderNumber, updatedBooking);
 196:
 197:                        // Continue with booking flow
 198:                        var synthetic = mainAgentResponse with
 199:                        {
 200:                            Intent = IntentType.Booking,
 201:                            ExtractedData = updatedBooking,
 202:                            Metadata = new Dictionary<string, object>
 203:                            {
 204:                                ["riceValidated"] = true,
 205:                                ["validatedRiceName"] = selectedRice
 206:                            }
 207:                        };
 208:
 209:                        return await HandleBookingAsync(
 210:                            synthetic,
 211:                            originalMessage,
 212:                            state,
 213:                            cancellationToken);
 214:                    }
 215:                }
 216:                else
 217:                {
 218:                    // User's response didn't match any option, ask again
 219:                    _logger.LogInformation("Could not parse rice selection from: {Message}", originalMessage.MessageText);
 220:                    var formattedOptions = FormatRiceOptionsForSelection(pendingRice.Options);
 221:                    return new AgentResponse
 222:                    {
 223:                        Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 224:                        AiResponse = ResponseVariations.RiceSelectionNotUnderstood(formattedOptions)
 225:                    };
 226:                }
 227:            }
 228:
 229:            // If we have a pending BOOKING_REQUEST from a previous turn, we can keep progressing
 230:            // even if the AI doesn't re-emit BOOKING_REQUEST on follow-up messages.
 231:            // This is critical when we ask mandatory follow-up questions (rice decision/servings, tronas, carritos).
 232:            if (mainAgentResponse.Intent != IntentType.Booking)
 233:            {
 234:                var pending = _pendingBookingStore.Get(originalMessage.SenderNumber);
 235:                if (pending != null && ShouldContinuePendingBooking(mainAgentResponse, originalMessage.MessageText))
 236:                {
 237:                    var synthetic = mainAgentResponse with
 238:                    {
 239:                        Intent = IntentType.Booking,
 240:                        ExtractedData = pending
 241:                    };
 242:
 243:                    return await HandleBookingAsync(
 244:                        synthetic,
 245:                        originalMessage,
 246:                        state,
 247:                        cancellationToken);
 248:                }
 249:            }
 250:
 251:            return mainAgentResponse.Intent switch
 252:            {
 253:                IntentType.Booking => await HandleBookingAsync(
 254:                    mainAgentResponse, originalMessage, state, cancellationToken),
 255:
 256:                // Note: Cancellation is handled earlier via state check, this is fallback
 257:                IntentType.Cancellation => await HandleCancellationAsync(
 258:                    mainAgentResponse, originalMessage, null, cancellationToken),
 259:
 260:                // Note: Modification is handled earlier via state check, this is fallback
 261:                IntentType.Modification => await HandleModificationAsync(
 262:                    mainAgentResponse, originalMessage, null, cancellationToken),
 263:
 264:                IntentType.ReservationQuery => await HandleReservationQueryAsync(
 265:                    mainAgentResponse, originalMessage, cancellationToken),
 266:
 267:                IntentType.SameDay => HandleSameDay(mainAgentResponse),
 268:
 269:                IntentType.Interactive => HandleInteractive(mainAgentResponse),
 270:
 271:                IntentType.Error => HandleError(mainAgentResponse),
 272:
 273:                _ => mainAgentResponse // Normal - return as-is
 274:            };
 275:        }
 276:        catch (Exception ex)
 277:        {
 278:            _logger.LogError(ex, "Error routing intent {Intent}", mainAgentResponse.Intent);
 279:            return AgentResponse.Error("Error processing your request");
 280:        }
 281:    }
 282:
 283:    private static bool ShouldContinuePendingBooking(AgentResponse mainAgentResponse, string userText)
 284:    {
 285:        // Respect explicit non-booking intents from the classifier/router.
 286:        if (mainAgentResponse.Intent is IntentType.Cancellation or
 287:            IntentType.Modification or
 288:            IntentType.ReservationQuery or
 289:            IntentType.SameDay or
 290:            IntentType.Interactive or
 291:            IntentType.Error)
 292:        {
 293:            return false;
 294:        }
 295:
 296:        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userText))
 297:            return false;
 298:
 299:        // If user is clearly switching intent, don't force booking continuation.
 300:        if (System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(
 301:                userText,
 302:                @"\b(cancelar|anular|modificar|cambiar|mis\s+reservas?|ver\s+reservas?)\b",
 303:                System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase))
 304:        {
 305:            return false;
 306:        }
 307:
 308:        // Continue pending booking only for messages that look like booking follow-up data.
 309:        return System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(
 310:            userText,
 311:            @"\b(sí|si|no|vale|ok|perfecto|confirmo|confirmar|personas?|comensales?|trona|tronas|carrito|carritos|arroz|raciones?|sin\s+arroz|a\s+las|\d{1,2}:\d{2}|\d{1,2}/\d{1,2}|lunes|martes|miércoles|miercoles|jueves|viernes|sábado|sabado|domingo|mañana|pasado\s+mañana)\b",
 312:            System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
 313:    }
 314:
 315:    #region Intent Handlers
 316:
 317:    private async Task<AgentResponse> HandleBookingAsync(
 318:        AgentResponse response,
 319:        WhatsAppMessage message,
 320:        ConversationState? state,
 321:        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
 322:    {
 323:        _logger.LogDebug("Handling booking intent");
 324:
 325:        // Store the latest extracted booking so we can resume on follow-up turns without requiring
 326:        // the AI to re-emit BOOKING_REQUEST.
 327:        if (response.ExtractedData != null)
 328:        {
 329:            _pendingBookingStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, response.ExtractedData);
 330:        }
 331:
 332:        // Check for large groups (>10 people) - redirect to reservations team
 333:        if (response.ExtractedData?.People > 10 || state?.Personas > 10)
 334:        {
 335:            var partySize = response.ExtractedData?.People ?? state?.Personas ?? 0;
 336:            _logger.LogInformation(
 337:                "Large group detected ({People} people) for {Phone}, sending contact card",
 338:                partySize, message.SenderNumber);
 339:
 340:            var whatsApp = _services.GetRequiredService<IWhatsAppService>();
 341:
 342:            // Send introductory message
 343:            await whatsApp.SendTextAsync(
 344:                message.SenderNumber,
 345:                ResponseVariations.LargeGroupIntro(),
 346:                cancellationToken);
 347:
 348:            // Send contact card
 349:            await whatsApp.SendContactCardAsync(
 350:                message.SenderNumber,
 351:                fullName: "Gestión Reservas Villa Carmen",
 352:                contactPhoneNumber: "34638857294",
 353:                organization: "Alquería Villa Carmen",
 354:                cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
 355:
 356:            // Clear pending booking
 357:            _pendingBookingStore.Clear(message.SenderNumber);
 358:
 359:            return new AgentResponse
 360:            {
 361:                Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 362:                AiResponse = ResponseVariations.LargeGroupVCard()
 363:            };
 364:        }
 365:
 366:        // Check if we need to validate rice type first
 367:        var needsRiceValidation = response.ExtractedData?.ArrozType != null &&
 368:                                  (response.Metadata?.ContainsKey("riceValidated") != true);
 369:
 370:        if (needsRiceValidation && response.ExtractedData?.ArrozType != null)
 371:        {
 372:            var riceValidator = _services.GetRequiredService<RiceValidatorAgent>();
 373:            var riceResult = await riceValidator.ValidateAsync(
 374:                response.ExtractedData.ArrozType,
 375:                "villacarmen",
 376:                cancellationToken);
 377:
 378:            // Handle MULTIPLE matches - ask user to choose
 379:            if (riceResult.Status == "multiple" && riceResult.Options?.Count > 1)
 380:            {
 381:                _logger.LogInformation(
 382:                    "Multiple rice matches found: {Options}",
 383:                    string.Join(", ", riceResult.Options));
 384:
 385:                // Store pending rice selection
 386:                _pendingRiceStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, new PendingRiceSelection
 387:                {
 388:                    Options = riceResult.Options,
 389:                    OriginalRequest = response.ExtractedData.ArrozType
 390:                });
 391:
 392:                // Format options with numbers for easy selection
 393:                var formattedOptions = FormatRiceOptionsForSelection(riceResult.Options);
 394:
 395:                return new AgentResponse
 396:                {
 397:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 398:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.MultipleRiceOptionsSelection(formattedOptions),
 399:                    Metadata = new Dictionary<string, object>
 400:                    {
 401:                        ["riceValidation"] = riceResult,
 402:                        ["pendingRiceSelection"] = true
 403:                    }
 404:                };
 405:            }
 406:
 407:            if (!riceResult.IsValid)
 408:            {
 409:                _logger.LogInformation(
 410:                    "Rice validation failed: {Status}",
 411:                    riceResult.Status);
 412:
 413:                return new AgentResponse
 414:                {
 415:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 416:                    AiResponse = riceResult.Message ?? ResponseVariations.RiceTypeNotFound(),
 417:                    Metadata = new Dictionary<string, object>
 418:                    {
 419:                        ["riceValidation"] = riceResult
 420:                    }
 421:                };
 422:            }
 423:
 424:            // Update booking data with validated rice name (full database name)
 425:            response = response with
 426:            {
 427:                ExtractedData = response.ExtractedData with
 428:                {
 429:                    ArrozType = riceResult.RiceName
 430:                },
 431:                Metadata = new Dictionary<string, object>(response.Metadata ?? new())
 432:                {
 433:                    ["riceValidated"] = true,
 434:                    ["validatedRiceName"] = riceResult.RiceName ?? ""
 435:                }
 436:            };
 437:        }
 438:
 439:        // Check availability
 440:        // If user confirmed and AI emitted BOOKING_REQUEST, we still enforce required fields in code
 441:        // before actually creating the booking.
 442:
 443:        // If we have all data, create the booking
 444:        if (response.ExtractedData?.IsValid == true)
 445:        {
 446:            if (state == null)
 447:            {
 448:                return response with
 449:                {
 450:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 451:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.IncompleteBookingPrompt()
 452:                };
 453:            }
 454:
 455:            // 0a) If time was rejected (availability issue), ask for new time before proceeding
 456:            if (state.InvalidHora)
 457:            {
 458:                _logger.LogInformation("Time was rejected, asking for new time");
 459:                _pendingBookingStore.Clear(message.SenderNumber);
 460:                return response with
 461:                {
 462:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 463:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskTime()
 464:                };
 465:            }
 466:
 467:            // 0b) If date was rejected (no capacity), ask for new date before proceeding
 468:            if (state.InvalidFecha)
 469:            {
 470:                _logger.LogInformation("Date was rejected (no availability), asking for new date");
 471:                _pendingBookingStore.Clear(message.SenderNumber);
 472:                return response with
 473:                {
 474:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 475:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskDate()
 476:                };
 477:            }
 478:
 479:            // 1) Enforce mandatory "extras" collection (must be asked & answered)
 480:            // Use combined question for more fluid conversation
 481:            if (state.HighChairs is null && state.BabyStrollers is null)
 482:            {
 483:                return response with
 484:                {
 485:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 486:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskTronasAndCarritos()
 487:                };
 488:            }
 489:
 490:            if (state.HighChairs is null)
 491:            {
 492:                return response with
 493:                {
 494:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 495:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskTronas()
 496:                };
 497:            }
 498:
 499:            // User said "sí" but didn't specify how many yet (we store -1)
 500:            if (state.HighChairs.Value < 0)
 501:            {
 502:                return response with
 503:                {
 504:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 505:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskTronasCount()
 506:                };
 507:            }
 508:
 509:            if (state.HighChairs.Value > 3)
 510:            {
 511:                return response with
 512:                {
 513:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 514:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.MaxTronas()
 515:                };
 516:            }
 517:
 518:            if (state.BabyStrollers is null)
 519:            {
 520:                return response with
 521:                {
 522:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 523:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskCarritos()
 524:                };
 525:            }
 526:
 527:            if (state.BabyStrollers.Value < 0)
 528:            {
 529:                return response with
 530:                {
 531:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 532:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskCarritosCount()
 533:                };
 534:            }
 535:
 536:            if (state.BabyStrollers.Value > 3)
 537:            {
 538:                return response with
 539:                {
 540:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 541:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.MaxCarritos()
 542:                };
 543:            }
 544:
 545:            // 2) Enforce rice flow rules
 546:            // Prefer validated rice name from ExtractedData when rice was validated
 547:            var riceWasValidated = response.Metadata?.ContainsKey("riceValidated") == true &&
 548:                                   response.Metadata["riceValidated"] is bool validated && validated;
 549:            var arrozType = riceWasValidated && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(response.ExtractedData?.ArrozType)
 550:                ? response.ExtractedData.ArrozType
 551:                : state?.ArrozType;
 552:            var arrozServings = state?.ArrozServings;
 553:
 554:            // Not decided yet -> must ask
 555:            if (arrozType is null)
 556:            {
 557:                return response with
 558:                {
 559:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 560:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskRice()
 561:                };
 562:            }
 563:
 564:            // No rice
 565:            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(arrozType))
 566:            {
 567:                arrozType = null;
 568:                arrozServings = null;
 569:            }
 570:            else
 571:            {
 572:                // Rice chosen: servings required and minimum 2
 573:                if (!arrozServings.HasValue)
 574:                {
 575:                    return response with
 576:                    {
 577:                        Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 578:                        AiResponse = ResponseVariations.AskRicePortions()
 579:                    };
 580:                }
 581:
 582:                if (arrozServings.Value < 2)
 583:                {
 584:                    return response with
 585:                    {
 586:                        Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 587:                        AiResponse = ResponseVariations.MinRicePortions()
 588:                    };
 589:                }
 590:            }
 591:
 592:            // 3) Merge canonical data (name/phone from WhatsApp + extras from state)
 593:            var highChairsFinal = Math.Clamp(state!.HighChairs.GetValueOrDefault(0), 0, 3);
 594:            var babyStrollersFinal = Math.Clamp(state.BabyStrollers.GetValueOrDefault(0), 0, 3);
 595:
 596:            // Use validated rice name from arrozType (already resolved above to prefer ExtractedData)
 597:            // Note: ExtractedData is guaranteed non-null here due to IsValid check at line 412
 598:            var booking = response.ExtractedData! with
 599:            {
 600:                Name = message.PushName,
 601:                Phone = message.SenderNumber,
 602:                ArrozType = arrozType,  // Uses validated name if riceWasValidated
 603:                ArrozServings = arrozServings,
 604:                HighChairs = highChairsFinal,
 605:                BabyStrollers = babyStrollersFinal
 606:            };
 607:
 608:            _logger.LogInformation(
 609:                "Creating booking with rice: {ArrozType} (validated={Validated})",
 610:                arrozType ?? "(none)", riceWasValidated);
 611:
 612:            // 4) DB-backed availability checks (mirror PHP scripts)
 613:            if (TryParseDate(booking.Date, out var date) &&
 614:                TryParseTime(booking.Time, out var time))
 615:            {
 616:                var availabilityService = _services.GetRequiredService<IBookingAvailabilityService>();
 617:                var decision = await availabilityService.EvaluateAsync(
 618:                    date, booking.People, time, cancellationToken);
 619:
 620:                if (!decision.IsAvailable && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(decision.Message))
 621:                {
 622:                    // Clear the pending booking with invalid time/date to prevent loops
 623:                    // The user needs to provide new valid data
 624:                    if (decision.Reason == "hour_unavailable" || decision.Reason == "closed_day" || decision.Reason == "daily_full" || decision.Reason == "same_day")
 625:                    {
 626:                        _pendingBookingStore.Clear(message.SenderNumber);
 627:                        _logger.LogInformation(
 628:                            "Cleared pending booking for {Phone} due to availability issue: {Reason}",
 629:                            message.SenderNumber, decision.Reason);
 630:                    }
 631:
 632:                    // Same-day bookings: send intro message + contact card
 633:                    if (decision.Reason == "same_day")
 634:                    {
 635:                        var whatsApp = _services.GetRequiredService<IWhatsAppService>();
 636:
 637:                        await whatsApp.SendTextAsync(
 638:                            message.SenderNumber,
 639:                            ResponseVariations.SameDayBookingIntro(),
 640:                            cancellationToken);
 641:
 642:                        await whatsApp.SendContactCardAsync(
 643:                            message.SenderNumber,
 644:                            fullName: "Gestión Reservas Villa Carmen",
 645:                            contactPhoneNumber: "34638857294",
 646:                            organization: "Alquería Villa Carmen",
 647:                            cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
 648:                    }
 649:
 650:                    return new AgentResponse
 651:                    {
 652:                        Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 653:                        AiResponse = decision.Message
 654:                    };
 655:                }
 656:            }
 657:
 658:            // 5) CONFIRMATION SUMMARY - Show summary and require explicit confirmation
 659:            // Only proceed to booking creation if user has seen the summary
 660:            if (!booking.SummaryShown)
 661:            {
 662:                _logger.LogInformation(
 663:                    "All data collected for {Phone}, showing confirmation summary",
 664:                    message.SenderNumber);
 665:
 666:                // Store booking with SummaryShown flag
 667:                var bookingWithSummary = booking with { SummaryShown = true };
 668:                _pendingBookingStore.Set(message.SenderNumber, bookingWithSummary);
 669:
 670:                // Build and return the summary
 671:                var summary = ResponseVariations.BuildBookingSummary(
 672:                    booking.Date,
 673:                    booking.Time,
 674:                    booking.People,
 675:                    arrozType,
 676:                    arrozServings,
 677:                    highChairsFinal,
 678:                    babyStrollersFinal);
 679:
 680:                return new AgentResponse
 681:                {
 682:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
 683:                    AiResponse = summary,
 684:                    Metadata = new Dictionary<string, object>
 685:                    {
 686:                        ["awaitingConfirmation"] = true
 687:                    }
 688:                };
 689:            }
 690:
 691:            // 6) User has seen summary and confirmed - create the booking
 692:            var bookingHandler = _services.GetRequiredService<BookingHandler>();
 693:            var created = await bookingHandler.CreateBookingAsync(
 694:                booking,
 695:                message,
 696:                cancellationToken);
 697:
 698:            if (created.Metadata != null &&
 699:                created.Metadata.TryGetValue("bookingCreated", out var createdObj) &&
 700:                createdObj is bool createdBool &&
 701:                createdBool)
 702:            {
 703:                _pendingBookingStore.Clear(message.SenderNumber);
 704:            }
 705:
 706:            return created;
 707:        }
 708:
 709:        // Return the main response (still collecting data)
 710:        return response;
 711:    }
 712:
 713:    private static bool TryParseDate(string dateStr, out DateTime date)
 714:    {
 715:        date = default;
 716:        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dateStr)) return false;
 717:
 718:        return DateTime.TryParseExact(dateStr, "dd/MM/yyyy", null,
 719:                   System.Globalization.DateTimeStyles.None, out date) ||
 720:               DateTime.TryParseExact(dateStr, "d/M/yyyy", null,
 721:                   System.Globalization.DateTimeStyles.None, out date);
 722:    }
 723:
 724:    private static bool TryParseTime(string timeStr, out TimeSpan time)
 725:    {
 726:        time = default;
 727:        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(timeStr)) return false;
 728:
 729:        return TimeSpan.TryParseExact(timeStr, @"hh\:mm", null, out time) ||
 730:               TimeSpan.TryParseExact(timeStr, @"h\:mm", null, out time);
 731:    }
 732:
 733:    private async Task<AgentResponse> HandleCancellationAsync(
 734:        AgentResponse response,
 735:        WhatsAppMessage message,
 736:        CancellationState? currentState,
 737:        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
 738:    {
 739:        _logger.LogDebug(
 740:            "Handling cancellation intent (stage={Stage})",
 741:            currentState?.Stage.ToString() ?? "New");
 742:
 743:        var cancellationHandler = _services.GetRequiredService<CancellationHandler>();
 744:        return await cancellationHandler.ProcessCancellationAsync(
 745:            message,
 746:            currentState,
 747:            cancellationToken);
 748:    }
 749:
 750:    private async Task<AgentResponse> HandleModificationAsync(
 751:        AgentResponse response,
 752:        WhatsAppMessage message,
 753:        ModificationState? currentState,
 754:        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
 755:    {
 756:        _logger.LogDebug(
 757:            "Handling modification intent (stage={Stage})",
 758:            currentState?.Stage.ToString() ?? "New");
 759:
 760:        var modificationHandler = _services.GetRequiredService<ModificationHandler>();
 761:        return await modificationHandler.ProcessModificationAsync(
 762:            message,
 763:            currentState,
 764:            cancellationToken);
 765:    }
 766:
 767:    private async Task<AgentResponse> HandleReservationQueryAsync(
 768:        AgentResponse response,
 769:        WhatsAppMessage message,
 770:        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
 771:    {
 772:        _logger.LogInformation("Handling reservation query for {Phone}", message.SenderNumber);
 773:
 774:        try
 775:        {
 776:            var bookingRepo = _services.GetRequiredService<IBookingRepository>();
 777:            var bookings = await bookingRepo.FindBookingsByPhoneAsync(message.SenderNumber, cancellationToken);
 778:
 779:            string replyMessage;
 780:            if (bookings.Count == 0)
 781:            {
 782:                replyMessage = "No he encontrado ninguna reserva a tu nombre. Si deseas hacer una nueva reserva, dime para qué día y cuántas personas.";
 783:            }
 784:            else if (bookings.Count == 1)
 785:            {
 786:                var b = bookings[0];
 787:                var arrozInfo = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b.ArrozType) 
 788:                    ? $"\n🍚 Arroz: {b.ArrozType}" + (b.ArrozServings.HasValue ? $" ({b.ArrozServings} raciones)" : "")
 789:                    : "";
 790:                replyMessage = $"Tienes una reserva confirmada:\n\n📅 {b.DayName} {b.DateFormatted}\n🕒 {b.TimeFormatted}\n👥 {b.PartySize} personas{arrozInfo}\n\n¿Necesitas modificar algo?";
 791:            }
 792:            else
 793:            {
 794:                var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
 795:                sb.AppendLine($"Tienes {bookings.Count} reservas confirmadas:\n");
 796:                for (int i = 0; i < bookings.Count; i++)
 797:                {
 798:                    var b = bookings[i];
 799:                    var arrozInfo = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b.ArrozType) 
 800:                        ? $" | 🍚 {b.ArrozType}" + (b.ArrozServings.HasValue ? $" x{b.ArrozServings}" : "")
 801:                        : "";
 802:                    sb.AppendLine($"{i + 1}. {b.DayName} {b.DateFormatted} a las {b.TimeFormatted} | 👥 {b.PartySize}{arrozInfo}");
 803:                }
 804:                sb.AppendLine("\n¿Cuál quieres modificar o cancelar?");
 805:                replyMessage = sb.ToString();
 806:            }
 807:
 808:            return response with { AiResponse = replyMessage };
 809:        }
 810:        catch (Exception ex)
 811:        {
 812:            _logger.LogError(ex, "Error querying reservations for {Phone}", message.SenderNumber);
 813:            return response with { AiResponse = "Lo siento, ha habido un problema al consultar tus reservas. Por favor, llámanos al +34 638 857 294." };
 814:        }
 815:    }
 816:
 817:    private AgentResponse HandleSameDay(AgentResponse response)
 818:    {
 819:        _logger.LogDebug("Handling same-day booking rejection");
 820:
 821:        var message = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(response.AiResponse)
 822:            ? ResponseVariations.SameDayBookingRejection()
 823:            : response.AiResponse;
 824:
 825:        return response with { AiResponse = message };
 826:    }
 827:
 828:    private AgentResponse HandleInteractive(AgentResponse response)
 829:    {
 830:        _logger.LogDebug("Handling interactive response with URLs");
 831:
 832:        // The response already contains URLs, just pass through
 833:        // The WhatsApp service will format them appropriately
 834:        return response;
 835:    }
 836:
 837:    private AgentResponse HandleError(AgentResponse response)
 838:    {
 839:        _logger.LogWarning("Handling error response");
 840:
 841:        var message = ResponseVariations.GenericError();
 842:
 843:        return response with { AiResponse = message };
 844:    }
 845:
 846:    #endregion
 847:
 848:    #region Rice Selection Helpers
 849:
 850:    /// <summary>
 851:    /// Tries to parse user's rice selection from their message.
 852:    /// Supports: numbers (1, 2), ordinals (la primera, la segunda), partial names.
 853:    /// </summary>
 854:    private static string? TryParseRiceSelection(string userMessage, List<string> options)
 855:    {
 856:        var text = userMessage.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
 857:
 858:        // Direct number selection: "1", "2", etc.
 859:        if (int.TryParse(text, out var num) && num >= 1 && num <= options.Count)
 860:        {
 861:            return options[num - 1];
 862:        }
 863:
 864:        // Ordinal selection: "la primera", "la 1", "la segunda", "la 2"
 865:        var ordinalPatterns = new Dictionary<string, int>
 866:        {
 867:            [@"^(la\s+)?primer[ao]?$"] = 1,
 868:            [@"^(la\s+)?1$"] = 1,
 869:            [@"^(el\s+)?1$"] = 1,
 870:            [@"^(la\s+)?segund[ao]?$"] = 2,
 871:            [@"^(la\s+)?2$"] = 2,
 872:            [@"^(el\s+)?2$"] = 2,
 873:            [@"^(la\s+)?tercer[ao]?$"] = 3,
 874:            [@"^(la\s+)?3$"] = 3,
 875:            [@"^(el\s+)?3$"] = 3,
 876:            [@"^(la\s+)?cuart[ao]?$"] = 4,
 877:            [@"^(la\s+)?4$"] = 4,
 878:            [@"^(el\s+)?4$"] = 4
 879:        };
 880:
 881:        foreach (var (pattern, index) in ordinalPatterns)
 882:        {
 883:            if (System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(text, pattern) && index <= options.Count)
 884:            {
 885:                return options[index - 1];
 886:            }
 887:        }
 888:
 889:        // Partial name match: "la de Albufera", "la del pato", "paella valenciana"
 890:        foreach (var option in options)
 891:        {
 892:            var optionLower = option.ToLowerInvariant();
 893:            // Check if user message contains significant words from option
 894:            var significantWords = ExtractSignificantWords(optionLower);
 895:            var userWords = ExtractSignificantWords(text);
 896:
 897:            // If user mentions at least one significant word that uniquely identifies this option
 898:            foreach (var word in userWords)
 899:            {
 900:                if (word.Length >= 4 && optionLower.Contains(word))
 901:                {
 902:                    // Check this word doesn't appear in other options
 903:                    var matchCount = options.Count(o => o.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(word));
 904:                    if (matchCount == 1)
 905:                    {
 906:                        return option;
 907:                    }
 908:                }
 909:            }
 910:        }
 911:
 912:        // Check for "de la" pattern: "la de la Albufera"
 913:        var delaMatch = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(text, @"(?:la\s+)?de\s+(?:la\s+)?(\w+)");
 914:        if (delaMatch.Success)
 915:        {
 916:            var keyword = delaMatch.Groups[1].Value;
 917:            foreach (var option in options)
 918:            {
 919:                if (option.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(keyword))
 920:                {
 921:                    return option;
 922:                }
 923:            }
 924:        }
 925:
 926:        return null;
 927:    }
 928:
 929:    /// <summary>
 930:    /// Extracts significant words (ignoring common articles and prepositions).
 931:    /// </summary>
 932:    private static HashSet<string> ExtractSignificantWords(string text)
 933:    {
 934:        var stopWords = new HashSet<string>
 935:        {
 936:            "el", "la", "los", "las", "un", "una", "de", "del", "con", "y", "por", "para",
 937:            "arroz", "paella", "fideua", "fideuá", "encargo", "€"
 938:        };
 939:
 940:        return text.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
 941:            .Where(w => w.Length >= 3 && !stopWords.Contains(w))
 942:            .Select(w => System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(w, @"[^\w]", ""))
 943:            .Where(w => w.Length >= 3)
 944:            .ToHashSet();
 945:    }
 946:
 947:    /// <summary>
 948:    /// Formats rice options with numbers for display.
 949:    /// </summary>
 950:    private static string FormatRiceOptionsForSelection(List<string> options)
 951:    {
 952:        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
 953:        for (int i = 0; i < options.Count; i++)
 954:        {
 955:            sb.AppendLine($"{i + 1}. {options[i]}");
 956:        }
 957:        return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();
 958:    }
 959:
 960:    #endregion
 961:
 962:    #region Rice Offer Response Detection
 963:
 964:    /// <summary>
 965:    /// Checks if the user is responding to a rice offer message for an existing reservation.
 966:    /// Returns a response if this is a rice offer rejection, null otherwise.
 967:    /// </summary>
 968:    private async Task<AgentResponse?> CheckRiceOfferResponseAsync(
 969:        WhatsAppMessage message,
 970:        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
 971:    {
 972:        try
 973:        {
 974:            var historyService = _services.GetRequiredService<IConversationHistoryService>();
 975:            var history = await historyService.GetHistoryAsync(message.SenderNumber, cancellationToken);
 976:
 977:            if (history == null || history.Count == 0)
 978:                return null;
 979:
 980:            // Get the last bot message
 981:            var lastBotMessage = history
 982:                .Where(m => m.Role == "assistant")
 983:                .LastOrDefault()?.Content ?? "";
 984:
 985:            // Check if the last bot message was a rice offer
 986:            var isRiceOfferMessage = TurnAnalysis.TurnClassifier.IsRiceOfferMessage(lastBotMessage);
 987:
 988:            if (!isRiceOfferMessage)
 989:                return null;
 990:
 991:            // Check if the user is declining the rice offer
 992:            var isRejection = TurnAnalysis.TurnClassifier.IsRiceOfferDecline(message.MessageText);
 993:
 994:            if (isRejection)
 995:            {
 996:                _logger.LogInformation(
 997:                    "User declined rice offer for existing reservation: {Message}",
 998:                    message.MessageText);
 999:
1000:                return new AgentResponse
1001:                {
1002:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
1003:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.RiceOfferDeclined()
1004:                };
1005:            }
1006:        }
1007:        catch (Exception ex)
1008:        {
1009:            _logger.LogError(ex, "Error checking rice offer response");
1010:        }
1011:
1012:        return null;
1013:    }
1014:
1015:    #endregion
1016:}]]>

38:### Root User Step Restoration Architecture

Base atoms track mutable state including <code>currentStepAtom</code> (active step 1-10), <code>completedStepsAtom</code> (Set of finished step numbers), <code>stepEditHistoryAtom</code> (timestamp records of root user edits), <code>targetRegenerationStepAtom</code> (pending regeneration target), and <code>showRootNotificationAtom</code> (notification visibility toggle). Supporting utilities in <span class="code-ref">src/pages/ArticleWriter/utils/stepNavigationUtils.ts</span> provide 20+ helper functions for non-React contexts, validation logic (step number ranges, transition validity), data management (step metadata, progress calculation), and localStorage persistence operations.

 63:    public async Task<AgentResponse> ProcessAsync(

 68:        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
 69:    {

 71:        {

 76:                    ? message.MessageText[..50] + "..."

 80:            var restaurantId = GetRestaurantId(message.SenderNumber);
 81:
 82:            // 2. Get conversation history if not provided
 83:            history ??= await _historyService.GetHistoryAsync(
 84:                message.SenderNumber, cancellationToken);
 85:
 86:            // 3. Extract conversation state if not provided
 87:            state ??= _historyService.ExtractState(history);

 90:            var riceValidation = await PreValidateRiceAsync(message.MessageText, restaurantId, cancellationToken);
 91:
 92:            if (riceValidation.HasRiceRequest && !riceValidation.IsValid)
 93:            {
 94:                // Invalid rice - return rejection message directly
 95:                _logger.LogInformation(
 96:                    "Rice pre-validation failed: {RiceRequest} not found in menu",
 97:                    riceValidation.RequestedRice);
 98:
 99:                return new AgentResponse
100:                {
101:                    Intent = IntentType.Normal,
102:                    AiResponse = ResponseVariations.RiceNotOnMenu(
103:                        riceValidation.RequestedRice ?? "",
104:                        string.Join(", ", riceValidation.AvailableTypes))
105:                };
106:            }
107:
108:            // 4. Build context with all dynamic values (including existing bookings)
109:            var context = _contextBuilder.BuildContext(message, state, history, existingBookings);
110:
111:            // 4b. Retrieve long-term semantic memory from vector store (isolated by phone)
112:            if (_vectorStore != null)
113:            {
114:                try
115:                {
116:                    var topK = Math.Max(1, _configuration.GetValue("Chroma:TopK", 6));
117:                    var semanticMemory = await _vectorStore.QueryRelevantAsync(
118:                        message.SenderNumber,
119:                        message.MessageText,
120:                        topK,
121:                        cancellationToken);
122:
123:                    if (semanticMemory.Count > 0)
124:                    {
125:                        context["hasSemanticContext"] = true;
126:                        context["semanticContext"] = _contextBuilder.FormatHistory(semanticMemory, topK);
127:                    }
128:                    else
129:                    {
130:                        context["hasSemanticContext"] = false;
131:                        context["semanticContext"] = "";
132:                    }
133:                }
134:                catch (Exception ex)
135:                {
136:                    _logger.LogWarning(ex, "Failed to retrieve semantic context from vector store");
137:                    context["hasSemanticContext"] = false;
138:                    context["semanticContext"] = "";
139:                }
140:            }
141:            else
142:            {
143:                context["hasSemanticContext"] = false;

145:            }
146:
147:            // Add rice validation result to context if valid

169:            _logger.LogDebug(

173:            // 7. Parse the response for commands and clean it
174:            var parsedResponse = ParseAiResponse(aiResponse, message, state);

176:            // NOTE: History is now stored in WebhookController AFTER IntentRouter
177:            // processes the response. This ensures the FINAL response (which may be
178:            // modified by IntentRouter) is what gets stored, not the intermediate AI response.

180:            return parsedResponse;
181:        }
182:        catch (GeminiApiException ex)

187:        catch (Exception ex)

189:            _logger.LogError(ex, "Unexpected error processing message");
190:            return AgentResponse.Error("Unexpected error occurred");
191:        }
192:    }

196:    /// <summary>
197:    /// Gets the restaurant ID based on the sender's phone number.
198:    /// In production, this would look up from a database or configuration.
199:    /// </summary>
200:    private string GetRestaurantId(string senderNumber)
201:    {
202:        // Try to find mapping in configuration (be resilient to missing sections / mocks)
203:        var mappingSection = _configuration.GetSection("Restaurants:Mapping");
204:        var children = mappingSection?.GetChildren() ?? Enumerable.Empty<IConfigurationSection>();
205:        var mapping = children.ToDictionary(x => x.Key, x => x.Value);
206:
207:        if (mapping.TryGetValue(senderNumber, out var restaurantId))
208:        {
209:            return restaurantId ?? "villacarmen";
210:        }
211:
212:        // Fall back to default
213:        return _configuration["Restaurants:Default"] ?? "villacarmen";
214:    }

216:    /// <summary>

><![CDATA[ 1:## PROCESO DE MODIFICACIÓN
 2:
 3:### DETECCIÓN DE INTENCIÓN
 4:
 5:Cuando el usuario quiera MODIFICAR una reserva existente, responde brevemente y termina con:
 6:MODIFICATION_INTENT
 7:
 8:### FRASES QUE INDICAN MODIFICACIÓN
 9:
10:- "quiero cambiar/modificar mi reserva"
11:- "necesito cambiar la fecha/hora/personas"
12:- "¿puedo cambiar mi reserva?"
13:- "tengo que modificar algo"
14:- "cambio de planes"
15:- "al final seremos más/menos"
16:- "¿se puede cambiar la hora?"
17:- "quiero añadir/quitar arroz"
18:- "necesito más/menos tronas/carritos"
19:
20:### FORMATO DE RESPUESTA
21:
22:[Respuesta corta y amigable]
23:MODIFICATION_INTENT
24:
25:### ⚠️ IMPORTANTE: NO GENERES FORMATOS ESPECIALES AL CONFIRMAR
26:
27:Cuando el usuario CONFIRME la modificación:
28:- **NO** generes ningún comando como MODIFICATION_REQUEST|...
29:- **NO** pongas ningún formato especial al final
30:- Simplemente responde con un mensaje natural de confirmación como:
31:  - "¡Perfecto! Tu reserva ha sido modificada. ¡Te esperamos!"
32:  - "¡Hecho! Nos vemos pronto."
33:  - "¡Genial! La reserva está actualizada."
34:
35:El sistema procesará la modificación automáticamente. Tú solo responde de forma natural.
36:
37:**Correcto:**
38:¡Vale {{pushName}}! Vamos a modificar tu reserva.
39:MODIFICATION_INTENT
40:
41:Claro! Déjame ver tus reservas...
42:MODIFICATION_INTENT
43:
44:Por supuesto, voy a buscar tu reserva.
45:MODIFICATION_INTENT
46:
47:**Incorrecto:**
48:Claro, para modificar tu reserva necesito saber: ¿Para qué día era? ¿A qué hora? ¿Cuántas personas?
49:(NO preguntes todos los datos - el sistema los buscará automáticamente)
50:
51:### IMPORTANTE
52:
53:- **NO pidas** datos de la reserva original (fecha, hora, nombre)
54:- **SIEMPRE incluye** MODIFICATION_INTENT al final

Rigid stages: SelectingBooking → SelectingField → CollectingNewValue → AwaitingConfirmation\n   - Each stage expects specific input format\n   - No support for multi-field updates in single message\n\n2. **Single-Field Focus** (`ModificationHandler.cs:305-318`)\n   - Rationale: Prevents infinite loops of \"no entendí\"\n\n### Phase 5: Prompt Engineering for Natural Interaction\n\n**Objective**: Update AI prompts to support natural language modification\n\n- [ ] **Task 5.1**: Update `modification-flow.txt` prompt\n  - Rationale: Aligns AI behavior with new flexible flow\n\n- [ ] **Task 5.2**: Add natural language examples to prompt\n  ```\n  User: \"No es para el 13, es para el domingo 15 a las 14:30\"\n  Bot: \"Perfecto, cambio tu reserva al domingo 15 de marzo a las 14:30. ¿Confirmas?\"\n  \n  User: \"Quiero cambiar a más tarde\"\n  Bot: \"Vale, ¿a qué hora te viene mejor? Ahora tienes las 14:00\"\n  \n  User: \"Para 10 personas y a las 14:30\"\n  Bot: \"Entonces cambio a 10 personas y a las 14:30. ¿Confirmas?\"\n  ```\n  - Rationale: Provides AI with examples of natural conversation patterns\n\n- [ ] **Task 5.3**: Update `system-main.txt` with modification context awareness\n  - Add instruction to detect multi-field expressions\n  - Add instruction to maintain context across modification turns\n  - Add instruction to avoid repetitive questions\n  - Rationale: Improves AI's understanding of modification context\n\n### Phase 6: Availability Integration with Accumulated Fields\n\n**Objective**: Validate availability for multi-field changes atomically\n\n- [ ] **Task 6.1**: Create `MultiFieldAvailabilityValidator.cs` in `Services/` directory\n  - Implement `ValidateAccumulatedChangesAsync(booking, accumulatedChanges)` method\n  - Check availability for date+time combinations together\n  - Return consolidated availability result with suggestions\n  - Rationale: Prevents showing availability for wrong time/date combinations\n\n- [ ] **Task 6.2**: Update `HandleDateChangeAsync()` and `HandleTimeChangeAsync()`\n  - Check for accumulated time/date in state before validating\n  - Use accumulated time when checking date availability\n  - Use accumulated date when checking time availability\n  - Rationale: Ensures availability checks use complete information\n\n- [ ] **Task 6.3**: Implement smart suggestion builder\n  - If date+time not available → suggest closest available combinations\n  - Format: \"El domingo 15 a las 14:30 no está disponible, pero tengo a las 13:30 o a las 15:00\"\n  - Allow user to choose or provide alternative\n  - Rationale: Provides helpful alternatives instead of just rejection\n\n### Phase 7: Testing and Validation\n\n**Objective**: Ensure natural language understanding works correctly\n\n- [ ] **Task 7.1**: Create unit tests for `NaturalLanguageModificationParser`\n  - Test combined date+time extraction: \"domingo 15 a las 14:30\"\n  - Test partial field extraction: \"para 10 personas\"\n  - Test relative expressions: \"más tarde\"\n  - Test context-aware inference: \"14:30\" when asked for date\n  - Rationale: Validates parser handles various natural language patterns\n\n- [ ] **Task 7.2**: Create integration tests for validation with accumulated fields\n  - Rationale: Validates end-to-end flow works naturally\n\n- [ ] **Task 7.3**: Create conversation test scenarios in `testing/` directory\n  - Scenario: User provides date+time together\n  - Scenario: User provides partial information across turns\n  - Scenario: User corrects previous input\n  - Scenario: User provides natural expressions (\"más tarde\", \"somos 10\")\n  - Rationale: Validates real-world conversation patterns work\n\n- [ ] **Task 7.4**: Add logging for natural language understanding metrics\n  - Log extraction success rate\n  - Log average turns to complete modification\n  - Log clarification request frequency\n  - Rationale: Enables monitoring of improvement effectiveness\n\n## Verification Criteria\n\n### Functional Requirements\n\n- [ ] User can provide date and time in single message: \"domingo 15 a las 14:30\" → both fields extracted\n- [ ] System accumulates partial information: \"domingo 15\" → \"14:30\" → both fields recognized\n- [ ] No repetitive \"no entendí\" loops for valid natural expressions\n- [ ] Confirmation shows ALL fields being modified, not just one\n- [ ] Availability checks use complete date+time information\n- [ ] System handles corrections gracefully: \"no, es para el domingo\"\n\n### Performance Requirements\n\n- [ ] Average modification completion time reduced by 40% (fewer turns)\n- [ ] Clarification request rate reduced by 50%\n- [ ] User satisfaction score improved (measured via conversation analysis)\n\n### User Experience Requirements\n\n- [ ] No numbered menus shown during modification (unless user explicitly asks for options)\n- [ ] Natural language responses feel conversational, not robotic\n- [ ] System understands Spanish expressions naturally (\"más tarde\", \"somos X personas\")\n- [ ] Maximum 3 turns to complete simple modification (date OR time OR party size)\n- [ ] Maximum 5 turns to complete complex modification (date AND time AND party size)\n\n## Potential Risks and Mitigations\n\n### Risk 1: Over-Extraction of Fields\n**Description**: Parser extracts fields that user didn't intend to modify\n**Mitigation**: \n- Implement confidence scoring for extracted fields\n- Only extract fields explicitly mentioned or strongly implied\n- Use conversation context to validate extraction relevance\n- When in doubt, ask confirmation: \"¿Quieres cambiar también la hora?\"\n\n### Risk 2: Ambiguous Natural Language\n**Description**: User expressions could be interpreted multiple ways\n**Mitigation**: \n- Maintain conversation context to disambiguate\n- Use `LastAskedField` to understand response context\n- When truly ambiguous, ask natural clarification: \"¿Te refieres a la fecha o a la hora?\"\n- Track clarification patterns to improve parser over time\n\n### Risk 3: Breaking Existing Functionality\n**Description**: Changes to state machine could break existing modification flows\n**Mitigation**: \n- Implement changes incrementally with feature flags\n- Maintain backward compatibility with menu-driven flow\n- Extensive testing of edge cases\n- Gradual rollout with monitoring\n\n### Risk 4: AI Prompt Confusion\n**Description**: AI may generate conflicting instructions with new prompts\n**Mitigation**: \n- Clear separation of concerns: AI detects intent, code handles extraction\n- Explicit instructions in prompts about when to use MODIFICATION_INTENT\n- Test prompts with various conversation scenarios\n- Version control prompts for easy rollback\n\n### Risk 5: Performance Impact\n**Description**: Natural language parsing may add latency\n**Mitigation**: \n- Optimize parser with compiled regex patterns\n- Cache frequently used patterns\n- Async processing where possible\n- Performance testing with load simulation\n\n## Alternative Approaches\n\n### Alternative 1: AI-Only Extraction (No Code Parser)\n**Description**: Let AI extract all fields from natural language without dedicated parser\n**Trade-offs**:\n- ✅ More flexible, handles edge cases better\n- ✅ Less code to maintain\n- ❌ Slower (requires AI call for every message)\n- ❌ Less predictable, harder to debug\n- ❌ Higher cost (more AI API calls)\n**Recommendation**: Use hybrid approach - AI for intent detection, code parser for field extraction\n\n### Alternative 2: Slot-Filling Dialogue System\n**Description**: Implement formal slot-filling system with defined slots and validation rules\n**Trade-offs**:\n- ✅ More structured and predictable\n- ✅ Easier to add new fields\n- ❌ More complex to implement\n- ❌ May feel less natural than direct extraction\n- ❌ Overkill for current scope\n**Recommendation**: Consider for future if adding many more modification fields\n\n### Alternative 3: Voice-First NLU Library\n**Description**: Integrate specialized NLU library (Rasa, Dialogflow) for natural language understanding\n**Trade-offs**:\n- ✅ Purpose-built for this problem\n- ✅ Better NLU capabilities out of box\n- ❌ Significant integration effort\n- ❌ External dependency and cost\n- ❌ Overkill for WhatsApp text-based bot\n**Recommendation**: Not necessary - custom parser
